# A34-pill-bar-per-mode-r1

## Diff summary

- Expected: new Settings rows Show Delete button, Show New note button, and Slot dots; three bottom-bar containers; corner Delete/New note buttons; top-right swap button; count button height 32; dot button height 32; mode-specific pill content.
- Existing values: only the old pill-root Border becomes a transparent/margin host for the new bottom-bar containers (8 palettes). Its former capsule style is now on the center pager container. Existing pager children are nested under that container; no other changed values were found.
- Removed paths: old pill subtree replaced by the new container/pager subtree. Settings subtree paths shifted after row insertion; no unrelated control removals found.
- UNEXPECTED: none.
- Dump SHA256: `6693fbbd9f7f302e0a6b4ffc2de92499d15515a8cfed556b532f96dadc3a96ee`
